 522:         ShadowPosition nearTpUpdated = current.withNearTp(nearTp.state());
 523:         if (!nearTpUpdated.sameNearTp(current)) {
 524:             persistNearTpState(nearTpUpdated);
 525:         }
 526:         current = nearTpUpdated;
 527:         if (nearTp.transition()) {
 528:             log.info("FIX-11T Replay Near-TP: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, code={}, state={}, price={}, best={}, givebackPct={}, bearish1mStreak={}, detail={}",
 529:                     runId, current.positionId(), symbol, nearTp.code(), current.nearTpState(), price,
 530:                     current.nearTpBestPrice(), nearTp.givebackPercent(), current.nearTpBearishStreak(), nearTp.explanation());
 531:         }
 532:         if (nearTp.harvestEligible()) {
 533:             BigDecimal soldQty = current.quantity().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC);
 534:             BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.Evaluation minimumCheck =
 535:                     binanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.evaluate(symbol, soldQty, price);
 536:             if (!minimumCheck.executable()) {
 537:                 // Same as Production: a below-minimum Binance order is not simulated. Reset
 538:                 // to rejection monitoring and let all existing management continue unchanged.
 539:                 current = current.withNearTp(new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(

 542:                 persistNearTpState(current);
 543:                 log.info("FIX-11T Replay NEAR_TP_HARVEST_SKIPPED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, reason={}, requestedQty={}, requestedNotional={}, binanceMinimum={}; existing position management continues",

 545:                         minimumCheck.requestedNotional(), minimumCheck.minimumNotional());

 547:             }

 549:             BigDecimal soldCost = current.entryPrice().multiply(soldQty, MC);
 550:             BigDecimal partialPnl = proceeds.subtract(soldCost, MC);
 551:             BigDecimal partialPct = soldCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO : partialPnl

 557:             return new LivePriceEvaluation(ExitDecision.hold(), current, proceeds, partialPnl);

 559:         return new LivePriceEvaluation(ExitDecision.hold(), current);

 698:         }
 699:         BigDecimal proceeds = soldQty.multiply(signal.getLatestPrice(), MC);
 700:         BigDecimal soldCost = updated.entryPrice().multiply(soldQty, MC);
 701:         BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(soldCost, MC);
 702:         BigDecimal pnlPct = soldCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO : pnl.divide(soldCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 703:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
 704:         updated = updated.withPartialHarvest(soldQty, soldCost, pnl);
 705:         persistNearTpPartialHarvestAtSignal(runId, signal, updated, soldQty, proceeds, pnl, pnlPct, evaluation.explanation());
 706:         log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, soldQty={}, remainingQty={}, price={}, partialPnl={}",
 707:                 runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), soldQty, updated.quantity(), signal.getLatestPrice(), pnl);
 708:         return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, proceeds, pnl);
 709:     }
 710: 
 711:     private ShadowPosition updateProfitLock(ShadowPosition p, TradeSignal s) {
 712:         if (s.getLatestPrice() == null || !priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(s, p.entryTime())) return p;
 713:         ShadowPosition n = profitLockState(p, s.getLatestPrice());
 714:         if (!equalsNullable(p.highest(), n.highest()) || p.profitLockActive() != n.profitLockActive()
 715:                 || !equalsNullable(p.profitLockPrice(), n.profitLockPrice())
 716:                 || p.profitLockState() != n.profitLockState()
 717:                 || !java.util.Objects.equals(p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt(), n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt())) {
 718:             jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET highest_price_usdt=?, profit_lock_active=?, profit_lock_price_usdt=?, profit_lock_state=?, profit_lock_rebase_started_at=? WHERE id=?",
 719:                     n.highest(), n.profitLockActive(), n.profitLockPrice(), n.profitLockState().name(),
 720:                     n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt() == null ? null : Timestamp.from(n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt()), p.positionId());
 721:         }
 722:         return n;
 723:     }
 724: 
 725:     private ShadowPosition profitLockState(ShadowPosition p, BigDecimal price) {
 726:         WalletSettings settings = walletSettings();
 727:         BigDecimal activation = nvl(settings.getProfitLockActivationPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(70));
 728:         BigDecimal highest = p.highest() == null ? price : p.highest().max(price);
 729:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(p.entryPrice(), p.takeProfit(), highest);
 730: 
 731:         if (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 732:                 && progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 733:             // Replay parity with Production FIX-118: preserve the historical lock/legacy flag,
 734:             // but keep it non-executable until the new TP geometry earns activation again.
 735:             return p.withLock(highest, true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 736:                     p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt());
 737:         }
 738: 
 739:         // FIX-118 parity: when REBASE earns activation, rebuild the lock from the current
 740:         // TP geometry instead of retaining the superseded pre-extension lock.
 741:         boolean freshGeometryActivation = p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 742:         ProfitLockPolicy.State state = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 743:                 p.entryPrice(), p.takeProfit(), price, highest,
 744:                 freshGeometryActivation ? false : (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE || p.profitLockActive()),
 745:                 freshGeometryActivation ? null : p.profitLockPrice(),
 746:                 settings.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled(), activation,
 747:                 nvl(settings.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)),
 748:                 nvl(settings.getProfitLockTrailStepPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(10)));
 749:         ProfitLockState nextState = state.active() ? ProfitLockState.ACTIVE : ProfitLockState.INACTIVE;
 750:         return p.withLock(state.highestPrice(), state.active(), state.lockPrice(), nextState, null);
 751:     }
 752: 
 753:     private ShadowPosition rebaseForExtendedTarget(ShadowPosition p, BigDecimal newTarget, BigDecimal extensionPrice, Instant at) {
 754:         if (p == null || newTarget == null) return p;
 755:         BigDecimal oldTarget = p.takeProfit();
 756:         BigDecimal oldLock = p.profitLockPrice();
 757:         BigDecimal activation = nvl(walletSettings().getProfitLockActivationPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(70));
 758:         BigDecimal extensionHighest = p.highest() == null ? p.entryPrice() : p.highest();
 759:         if (extensionPrice != null && extensionPrice.compareTo(extensionHighest) > 0) extensionHighest = extensionPrice;
 760:         ShadowPosition withExtensionHigh = p.withLock(extensionHighest, p.profitLockActive(), p.profitLockPrice(),
 761:                 p.profitLockState(), p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt());
 762:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(p.entryPrice(), newTarget, extensionHighest);
 763:         if (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE) {
 764:             if (progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 765:                 ShadowPosition rebasing = withExtensionHigh.withLock(extensionHighest, true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 766:                         at == null ? Instant.now() : at);
 767:                 log.info("[FIX-120][REPLAY][TP_GEOMETRY_REBASE] positionId={}, previousState={}, newState={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, " +
 768:                                 "extensionPrice={}, highest={}, newGeometryProgressPct={}, oldLock={}, freshLock={}, activationPct={}, geometryRebased=true",
 769:                         p.positionId(), p.profitLockState(), rebasing.profitLockState(), oldTarget, newTarget, extensionPrice,
 770:                         extensionHighest, progress, oldLock, rebasing.profitLockPrice(), activation);
 771:                 return rebasing;
 772:             }
 773:             // FIX-120 Production/Replay parity: the old executable lock belongs to the
 774:             // superseded TP geometry. If the extended winner still qualifies, establish a
 775:             // fresh geometry baseline. Raw extensionPrice is current; extensionHighest is the
 776:             // historical-best seed; previousActive/previousLock are deliberately discarded.
 777:             WalletSettings settings = walletSettings();
 778:             BigDecimal currentForPolicy = extensionPrice == null ? extensionHighest : extensionPrice;
 779:             ProfitLockPolicy.State rebased = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 780:                     p.entryPrice(), newTarget, currentForPolicy, extensionHighest,
 781:                     false, null, settings.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled(), activation,
 782:                     nvl(settings.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)),
 783:                     nvl(settings.getProfitLockTrailStepPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(10)));
 784:             if (rebased.active()) {
 785:                 ShadowPosition active = withExtensionHigh.withLock(rebased.highestPrice(), true, rebased.lockPrice(), ProfitLockState.ACTIVE, null);
 786:                 log.info("[FIX-120][REPLAY][TP_GEOMETRY_REBASE] positionId={}, previousState={}, newState={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, " +
 787:                                 "extensionPrice={}, highest={}, newGeometryProgressPct={}, oldLock={}, freshLock={}, activationPct={}, geometryRebased=true",
 788:                         p.positionId(), p.profitLockState(), active.profitLockState(), oldTarget, newTarget, extensionPrice,
 789:                         rebased.highestPrice(), rebased.progressPercent(), oldLock, rebased.lockPrice(), activation);
 790:                 return active;
 791:             }
 792:             ShadowPosition rebasing = withExtensionHigh.withLock(rebased.highestPrice(), true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 793:                     at == null ? Instant.now() : at);
 794:             log.info("[FIX-120][REPLAY][TP_GEOMETRY_REBASE] positionId={}, previousState={}, newState={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, " +
 795:                             "extensionPrice={}, highest={}, newGeometryProgressPct={}, oldLock={}, freshLock={}, activationPct={}, geometryRebased=true",
 796:                     p.positionId(), p.profitLockState(), rebasing.profitLockState(), oldTarget, newTarget, extensionPrice,
 797:                     rebased.highestPrice(), rebased.progressPercent(), oldLock, rebasing.profitLockPrice(), activation);
 798:             return rebasing;
 799:         }
 800:         if (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE) {
 801:             return withExtensionHigh.withLock(extensionHighest, true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 802:                     p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt());
 803:         }
 804:         return withExtensionHigh;
 805:     }
 806: 
 807:     private BigDecimal progressPercent(BigDecimal entry, BigDecimal target, BigDecimal highest) {
 808:         if (entry == null || target == null || highest == null || target.compareTo(entry) <= 0) return BigDecimal.ZERO;
 809:         BigDecimal favorable = highest.subtract(entry);
 810:         if (favorable.signum() < 0) favorable = BigDecimal.ZERO;
 811:         return favorable.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
 812:                 .divide(target.subtract(entry, MC), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
 813:     }

 817:                 p.profitLockActive(), p.profitLockPrice(), p.profitLockState().name(),
 818:                 p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt() == null ? null : Timestamp.from(p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt()), p.positionId());
 819:     }

 841:     private String replayStage(int allocationPercent) {
 842:         if (allocationPercent <= 0) return "NONE";
 843:         if (allocationPercent < 50) return "SCOUT_ENTRY";
 844:         if (allocationPercent < 100) return "CONFIRMATION_ADD";
 845:         return "TREND_ADD";
 846:     }

 848:     private ShadowPosition addToPosition(long runId, ShadowPosition open, TradeSignal signal,
 849:                                          BigDecimal addedQty, BigDecimal addedCost, int addPercent) {
 850:         BigDecimal newQuantity = open.quantity().add(addedQty, MC);
 851:         BigDecimal newCost = open.cost().add(addedCost, MC);
 852:         BigDecimal newEntry = newCost.divide(newQuantity, 12, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
 853:         BigDecimal newStop = open.stopLoss();
 854:         if (signal.getStopLoss() != null && (newStop == null || signal.getStopLoss().compareTo(newStop) > 0)) {
 855:             newStop = signal.getStopLoss();
 856:         }
 857:         BigDecimal newTakeProfit = open.takeProfit();
 858:         if (signal.getTakeProfit() != null && (newTakeProfit == null || signal.getTakeProfit().compareTo(newTakeProfit) > 0)) {
 859:             newTakeProfit = signal.getTakeProfit();
 860:         }
 861:         int newPercent = Math.min(100, open.positionPercent() + addPercent);
 862:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 863:                 UPDATE wallet_position_test
 864:                 SET entry_price=?, quantity=?, total_cost_usdt=?, position_percent=?,
 865:                     stop_loss_usdt=?, take_profit_usdt=?
 866:                 WHERE id=? AND test_run_id=?
 867:                 """, newEntry, newQuantity, newCost, newPercent, newStop, newTakeProfit,
 868:                 open.positionId(), runId);
 869:         ShadowPosition updated = open.withAdd(newEntry, newQuantity, newCost, newPercent, newStop, newTakeProfit);
 870:         persistNearTpState(updated);
 871:         return updated;
 872:     }

 901:     private long openPosition(long runId,String symbol,TradeSignal s,ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice fresh,BigDecimal qty,BigDecimal budget,int pct){
 902:         org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder kh=new org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder();
 903:         jdbcTemplate.update(c->{var ps=c.prepareStatement("""
 904:             INSERT INTO wallet_position_test
 905:             (test_run_id,symbol,status,entry_time,entry_price,quantity,total_cost_usdt,position_percent,stop_loss_usdt,take_profit_usdt,highest_price_usdt)
 906:             VALUES (?,?,'OPEN',?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)""",java.sql.Statement.RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS);
 907:             ps.setLong(1,runId);ps.setString(2,symbol);ps.setTimestamp(3,Timestamp.from(fresh.observedAt()));ps.setBigDecimal(4,fresh.price());ps.setBigDecimal(5,qty);ps.setBigDecimal(6,budget);ps.setInt(7,pct);ps.setBigDecimal(8,s.getStopLoss());ps.setBigDecimal(9,s.getTakeProfit());ps.setBigDecimal(10,fresh.price());return ps;},kh);
 908:         if (kh.getKey() == null) throw new IllegalStateException("Could not create shadow position");
 909:         return kh.getKey().longValue();
 910:     }

 999:     private WalletSettings walletSettings() {
1000:         return walletSettingsRepository.findById(1L).orElseGet(() -> WalletSettings.builder()
1001:                 .id(1L)
1002:                 .dynamicProfitLockEnabled(true)
1003:                 .profitLockActivationPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(70))
1004:                 .profitLockInitialPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(40))
1005:                 .profitLockTrailStepPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(10))
1006:                 .build());
1007:     }

1009:     private BigDecimal nvl(BigDecimal value, BigDecimal fallback) { return value == null ? fallback : value; }
1010: 
1011:     private String profitLockConfigText() {
1012:         WalletSettings s = walletSettings();
1013:         return "Admin Profit Lock: activation=" + nvl(s.getProfitLockActivationPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(70)).stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString()
1014:                 + "%, initial=" + nvl(s.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)).stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString()
1015:                 + "%, trail=" + nvl(s.getProfitLockTrailStepPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(10)).stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString() + "%.";
1016:     }

1018:     private BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPercent(ShadowPosition position, BigDecimal remainingLegPnl) {
1019:         BigDecimal totalPnl = position.partialRealizedPnl().add(remainingLegPnl, MC);
1020:         BigDecimal totalInvestedCost = position.cost().add(position.partialHarvestCostBasis(), MC);
1021:         return totalInvestedCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO
1022:                 : totalPnl.divide(totalInvestedCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
1023:     }

1025:     private BigDecimal percentage(BigDecimal entry,BigDecimal exit){return exit.subtract(entry).multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)).divide(entry,8,RoundingMode.HALF_UP);}
1026:     private boolean bullish(SignalDecision d){return d==SignalDecision.BUY||d==SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;}

1060:                     nearTpHarvestedQuantity, partialRealizedPnl, partialHarvestCostBasis);
1061:         }
1062:         ShadowPosition withTakeProfit(BigDecimal tp) {
1063:             // FIX-117 Replay parity: TP extension preserves already-earned Near-TP protection
1064:             // exactly like Production. REJECTION_DETECTED keeps its state/best price but clears
1065:             // bearish persistence accumulated under the old geometry. INACTIVE and
1066:             // FAILURE_CONFIRMED keep the pre-FIX-117 reset behavior. Harvested state is terminal.
1067:             NearTpState state = nearTpState;
1068:             BigDecimal bestPrice = nearTpBestPrice;
1069:             int bearishStreak = nearTpBearishStreak;
1070:             Long lastOneMinuteSignalId = nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId;
1071: 
1072:             if (!nearTpHarvestUsed) {
1073:                 switch (nearTpState) {
1074:                     case INACTIVE, NEAR_TP_FAILURE_CONFIRMED -> {
1075:                         state = NearTpState.INACTIVE;
1076:                         bestPrice = null;
1077:                         bearishStreak = 0;
1078:                         lastOneMinuteSignalId = null;
1079:                     }
1080:                     case NEAR_TP_ARMED -> { }
1081:                     case NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED -> {
1082:                         bearishStreak = 0;
1083:                         lastOneMinuteSignalId = null;
1084:                     }
1085:                     case NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED -> { }
1086:                 }
1087:             }
1088: 
1089:             return new ShadowPosition(positionId, entryTime, entryPrice, quantity, cost, positionPercent, stopLoss, tp,
1090:                     highest, profitLockActive, profitLockPrice, profitLockState, profitLockRebaseStartedAt,
1091:                     entryScore, entryConfidence, entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
1092:                     state, bestPrice, bearishStreak, lastOneMinuteSignalId, nearTpHarvestUsed,
1093:                     nearTpHarvestedQuantity, partialRealizedPnl, partialHarvestCostBasis);
1094:         }
1095:         ShadowPosition withAdd(BigDecimal newEntry, BigDecimal newQuantity, BigDecimal newCost, int newPercent, BigDecimal newStop, BigDecimal newTakeProfit) {
1096:             NearTpState state = nearTpHarvestUsed ? nearTpState : NearTpState.INACTIVE;
1097:             return new ShadowPosition(positionId, entryTime, newEntry, newQuantity, newCost, newPercent, newStop, newTakeProfit,
1098:                     highest, profitLockActive, profitLockPrice, profitLockState, profitLockRebaseStartedAt,
1099:                     entryScore, entryConfidence, entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,

1101:                     nearTpHarvestUsed ? nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId : null, nearTpHarvestUsed, nearTpHarvestedQuantity,
1102:                     partialRealizedPnl, partialHarvestCostBasis);
1103:         }

 807:     private BigDecimal progressPercent(BigDecimal entry, BigDecimal target, BigDecimal highest) {
 808:         if (entry == null || target == null || highest == null || target.compareTo(entry) <= 0) return BigDecimal.ZERO;
 809:         BigDecimal favorable = highest.subtract(entry);
 810:         if (favorable.signum() < 0) favorable = BigDecimal.ZERO;
 811:         return favorable.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
 812:                 .divide(target.subtract(entry, MC), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
 813:     }

 452:     private PaperPosition closeFromProfitLock(
 453:             PaperPosition position,
 454:             TradeSignal signal,
 455:             DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation profitLock
 456:     ) {
 457:         BigDecimal exitPrice = signal.getLatestPrice();
 458:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
 459:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
 460:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
 461:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
 462:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 463: 
 464:         position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 465:         position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 466:         position.setStatus(PositionStatus.CLOSED);
 467:         position.setCloseReason("PROFIT_LOCK");
 468:         position.setExitReason(profitLock.explanation());
 469:         position.setExitSignal(signal);
 470:         position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 471:         PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
 472: 
 473:         // FIX-020: terminal position close consumes the opportunity that financed this
 474:         // position so old pre-exit evidence cannot immediately open a brand-new trade.
 475:         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), signal, "PROFIT_LOCK");
 476: 
 477:         try {
 478:             walletAutoExecutionService.executeProfitLock(signal, exitPrice, profitLock.lockPrice());
 479:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 480:             log.error("Dynamic Profit Lock wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", signal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
 481:         }
 482:         // FIX-028: profit-lock exits use the same immutable production audit trail.
 483:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, signal, "PROFIT_LOCK", profitLock.explanation());
 484:         return saved;
 485:     }

 118:     public List<PriceEvent> find(String rawSymbol, Instant startInclusive, Instant endInclusive) {
 119:         String symbol = rawSymbol == null ? "" : rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
 120:         return jdbcTemplate.query("""
 121:                 SELECT observed_at, price, source
 122:                 FROM market_price_event
 123:                 WHERE delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED') AND symbol = ? AND observed_at >= ? AND observed_at <= ?
 124:                 ORDER BY observed_at ASC, id ASC
 125:                 """, (rs, rowNum) -> new PriceEvent(
 126:                         rs.getTimestamp("observed_at").toInstant(),
 127:                         rs.getBigDecimal("price"), rs.getString("source")),
 128:                 symbol, Timestamp.from(startInclusive), Timestamp.from(endInclusive));
 129:     }

 133:     public java.util.Optional<PriceEvent> findLatestAtOrBefore(String rawSymbol, Instant reference) {
 134:         String symbol = rawSymbol == null ? "" : rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
 135:         List<PriceEvent> rows = jdbcTemplate.query("""
 136:                 SELECT observed_at, price, source
 137:                 FROM market_price_event

 139:                 ORDER BY observed_at DESC, id DESC
 140:                 LIMIT 1
 141:                 """, (rs, rowNum) -> new PriceEvent(
 142:                         rs.getTimestamp("observed_at").toInstant(),
 143:                         rs.getBigDecimal("price"), rs.getString("source")),
 144:                 symbol, Timestamp.from(reference));

 148:     /** FIX-132: known skipped/unconfirmed collector delivery must not silently
 149:      * become legacy candle-price fallback. Historical pre-collector windows retain
 150:      * their established fallback. This reads immutable evidence, never live claims. */
 151:     public void assertReplayEvidence(String symbol,Instant from,Instant to) {
 152:         Long pending=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND delivery_status='PENDING'",
 153:             Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
 154:         if(pending!=null && pending>0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 Replay contains unconfirmed Production price outcomes; reconcile before claiming parity");
 155:         // FIX-153 OFF Replay never queries collector-era activation tables.
 156:         if("OFF".equals(mode))return;
 157:         Long cutovers=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_cutover_history WHERE symbol=? AND cutover_at<=?",Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(to));
 158:         if(cutovers!=null && cutovers>0) {
 159:             Long applied=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED')",
 160:                 Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
 161:             if(applied==null || applied==0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 no applied price evidence in collector-era Replay window; signal-price fallback is not Production reproduction");
 162:         }
 163:     }

  20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
  21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
  22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
  23:  */
  24: @Component
  25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
  26: 
  27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
  28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
  29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
  30: 
  31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
  32: 
  33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
  34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
  38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
  40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
  41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
  43:                 .toList();
  44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
  45:         return new Scope();
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
  49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
  50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
  51:         State s=required();
  52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
  53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
  54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
  55:     }
  56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
  58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
  59:     }
  60:     public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
  61:     public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
  62:     public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
  63: 
  64:     public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
  65:     public long runId() { return required().runId; }
  66:     public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
  67:     public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
  68:     public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
  69: 
  70:     public void reference(Instant reference) { required().reference = reference; }
  71: 
  72:     /** FIX-056: expose the exact latest replay market-price event to the shared execution-price authority. */
  73:     public void referencePrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {
  74:         State s = required();
  75:         s.reference = observedAt;
  76:         s.latestPrice = new ReplayPrice(symbol, observedAt, price);
  77:     }
  78: 
  79:     public Optional<ReplayPrice> latestPrice() {
  80:         State s = required();
  81:         return Optional.ofNullable(s.latestPrice);
  82:     }
  83: 
  84:     /** FIX-112A: Replay-local execution ledger keyed to the exact triggering signal. */
  85:     public boolean entryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  86:         return active() && signalId != null && required().consumedSignalIds.contains(signalId);
  87:     }
  88: 
  89:     /** FIX-112A: record only after that exact shadow BUY (initial or add) executes. */
  90:     public void markEntryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  91:         if (signalId != null) required().consumedSignalIds.add(signalId);
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     /**
  95:      * Adds a freshly generated replay signal to the as-of context stream. Signal generation
  96:      * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
  97:      * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.
  98:      */
  99:     public void appendSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
 100:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 101:         State s = required();
 102:         s.signals.add(signal);
 103:         s.signals.sort(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 104:     }
 105: 
 106:     public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
 107:         State s = required();
 108:         Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
 109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();
 110:         return s.signals.stream()
 111:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 112:                 .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
 113:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
 114:                 .limit(limit)
 115:                 .toList();
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 119:         return recent(symbol, interval, reference, 1).stream().findFirst();
 120:     }
 121: 
 122:     /**
 123:      * FIX-141: Replay-side equivalent of
 124:      * TradeSignalRepository#findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTimeLessThanEqualAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc.
 125:      * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
 126:      * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
 127:      * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
 128:      */
 129:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
 130:         State s = required();
 131:         if (reference == null || candleOpenTimeBound == null) return Optional.empty();
 132:         return s.signals.stream()
 133:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 134:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
 135:                 .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
 136:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     public Optional<TradeSignal> previousBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 140:         State s = required();
 141:         return s.signals.stream()
 142:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 143:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference))
 144:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 145:     }
 146: 
 147:     public Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> currentOpportunity(String symbol, List<String> statuses) {
 148:         ExecutionOpportunity o = required().opportunity;
 149:         if (o == null || !symbol.equals(o.getSymbol())) return Optional.empty();
 150:         if (statuses != null && !statuses.isEmpty() && !statuses.contains(o.getStatus())) return Optional.empty();
 151:         return Optional.of(o);
 152:     }
 153: 
 154:     public ExecutionOpportunity saveOpportunity(ExecutionOpportunity opportunity) {
 155:         State s = required();
 156:         s.opportunity = opportunity;
 157:         if (s.opportunitySink != null) s.opportunitySink.accept(opportunity);
 158:         return opportunity;
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     public final class Scope implements AutoCloseable {
 162:         private boolean closed;
 163:         @Override public void close() {
 164:             if (!closed) {
 165:                 state.remove();
 166:                 closed = true;
 167:             }
 168:         }
 169:     }
 170: 
 171:     private State required() {
 172:         State s = state.get();
 173:         if (s == null) throw new IllegalStateException("No Administration execution replay scope is active");
 174:         return s;
 175:     }
 176: 
 177:     public record ReplayPrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {}
 178: 
 179:     private static final class State {
 180:         final long runId;
 181:         final List<TradeSignal> signals;
 182:         final Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink;
 183:         final ReplayLogicMode logicMode;
 184:         boolean fix122Enabled = true;
 185:         final java.util.Map<String,StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary> stops = new java.util.HashMap<>();
 186:         Instant reference;
 187:         ExecutionOpportunity opportunity;
 188:         ReplayPrice latestPrice;
 189:         final Set<Long> consumedSignalIds = new HashSet<>();
 190:         State(long runId, List<TradeSignal> signals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
 191:             this.runId = runId;
 192:             this.signals = new ArrayList<>(signals);
 193:             this.opportunitySink = opportunitySink;
 194:             this.logicMode = logicMode;
 195:         }
 196:     }
 197: }

 251:     private void evaluateNearTpProtection(WalletManagedPosition managed, BigDecimal price,
 252:                                           TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
 253:         // FIX-11T evidence is explicitly time-safe. Normal Production logic above keeps its
 254:         // existing signal lookup unchanged; Near-TP alone falls back to an at-or-before query
 255:         // if the repository's newest row is future-dated relative to this live evaluation.
 256:         Instant evaluatedAt = Instant.now();
 257:         oneMinute = latestEligibleSignal(managed.getSymbol(), "1m", oneMinute, evaluatedAt);
 258:         fiveMinute = latestEligibleSignal(managed.getSymbol(), "5m", fiveMinute, evaluatedAt);
 259: 
 260:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state = new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 261:                 managed.getNearTpState() == null ? NearTpState.INACTIVE : managed.getNearTpState(),
 262:                 managed.getNearTpBestPrice(), managed.getNearTpBearishStreak(),
 263:                 managed.getNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), managed.isNearTpHarvestUsed());
 264: 
 265:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation evaluation = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
 266:                 state, managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt(),
 267:                 price, evaluatedAt, oneMinute, fiveMinute);
 268: 
 269:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State next = evaluation.state();
 270:         boolean stateChanged = managed.getNearTpState() != next.nearTpState()
 271:                 || !sameDecimal(managed.getNearTpBestPrice(), next.bestPrice())
 272:                 || managed.getNearTpBearishStreak() != next.consecutiveBearishOneMinute()
 273:                 || !java.util.Objects.equals(managed.getNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), next.lastEvaluatedOneMinuteSignalId());
 274:         if (stateChanged) {
 275:             managed.setNearTpState(next.nearTpState());
 276:             managed.setNearTpBestPrice(next.bestPrice());
 277:             managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(next.consecutiveBearishOneMinute());
 278:             managed.setNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(next.lastEvaluatedOneMinuteSignalId());
 279:             managed.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 280:             managedPositionRepository.save(managed);
 281:         }
 282: 
 283:         if (evaluation.transition() || stateChanged) {
 284:             log.info("FIX-11T Near-TP evaluation: positionId={}, symbol={}, state={}, code={}, price={}, bestPrice={}, " +
 285:                             "tpProgressPct={}, givebackPct={}, bearish1mStreak={}, oneMinuteId={}, oneMinuteOriginal={}, " +
 286:                             "fiveMinuteId={}, fiveMinuteOriginal={}, detail={}",
 287:                     managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), next.nearTpState(), evaluation.code(), price, next.bestPrice(),
 288:                     evaluation.tpProgressPercent(), evaluation.givebackPercent(), next.consecutiveBearishOneMinute(),
 289:                     oneMinute == null ? null : oneMinute.getId(),
 290:                     oneMinute == null ? null : oneMinute.getOriginalDecision(),
 291:                     fiveMinute == null ? null : fiveMinute.getId(),
 292:                     fiveMinute == null ? null : fiveMinute.getOriginalDecision(),
 293:                     evaluation.explanation());
 294:         } else if (log.isDebugEnabled() && evaluation.code().startsWith("HOLD_")) {
 295:             log.debug("FIX-11T Near-TP hold: positionId={}, symbol={}, code={}, price={}, detail={}",
 296:                     managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), evaluation.code(), price, evaluation.explanation());
 297:         }
 298: 
 299:         if (!evaluation.harvestEligible()) return;
 300: 
 301:         WalletAutoExecutionService.PartialHarvestResult harvest = walletAutoExecutionService
 302:                 .executeNearTpPartialHarvest(managed.getSymbol(), price, evaluation.explanation());
 303:         if (!harvest.executed()) {
 304:             log.warn("FIX-11T Near-TP harvest not executed: positionId={}, symbol={}, status={}, price={}",
 305:                     managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), harvest.status(), price);
 306:             // If execution did not happen, return to rejection monitoring rather than leaving
 307:             // the state stranded in FAILURE_CONFIRMED. Idempotency still lives in wallet_trade.
 308:             managed.setNearTpState(NearTpState.NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED);
 309:             if ("BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM".equals(harvest.status())
 310:                     || "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE".equals(harvest.status())) {
 311:                 // The skipped order consumed the current confirmation pair. Requiring fresh
 312:                 // bearish evidence prevents a retry on every subsequent market-price tick.
 313:                 managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(0);
 314:             }
 315:             managed.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 316:             managedPositionRepository.save(managed);
 317:             return;
 318:         }
 319: 
 320:         // Keep paper_position quantity/P&L synchronized with the managed wallet lifecycle.
 321:         // Without this, a later terminal signal close would calculate P&L on pre-harvest quantity.
 322:         PaperPosition paper = paperPositionRepository.findBySymbolAndStatus(managed.getSymbol(), PositionStatus.OPEN).orElse(null);
 323:         if (paper != null) {
 324:             paper.setQuantity(harvest.remainingQuantity());
 325:             BigDecimal priorRealized = paper.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : paper.getRealizedPnl();
 326:             paper.setRealizedPnl(priorRealized.add(harvest.realizedPnlUsdt(), MC));
 327:             paperPositionRepository.save(paper);
 328:         }
 329: 
 330:         log.info("FIX-11T NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED: positionId={}, symbol={}, soldQty={}, remainingQty={}, " +
 331:                         "price={}, realizedPnl={}, realizedPnlPct={}",
 332:                 managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), harvest.soldQuantity(), harvest.remainingQuantity(),
 333:                 price, harvest.realizedPnlUsdt(), harvest.realizedPnlPercent());
 334:     }

 336:     private TradeSignal latestEligibleSignal(String symbol, String interval, TradeSignal newest, Instant evaluatedAt) {
 337:         if (newest != null && newest.getGeneratedAt() != null && !newest.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(evaluatedAt)) {
 338:             return newest;
 339:         }
 340:         return tradeSignalRepository
 341:                 .findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, evaluatedAt)
 342:                 .orElse(null);
 343:     }

 347:      *
 348:      * Only ARMED and REJECTION_DETECTED change from the pre-FIX-117 behavior. INACTIVE
 349:      * still resets against the new geometry. FAILURE_CONFIRMED also keeps the existing
 350:      * reset behavior because that persisted/transient lifecycle has a real interruption

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/DynamicProfitLockService.java:200-280 =====
 200:             explanation = "Profit Lock not active yet; best progress is "
 201:                     + progress.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP) + "% and activation starts at "
 202:                     + activation.stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString() + "%." + profileText;
 203:         }
 204: 
 205:         return new Evaluation(true, executableActive, triggered, position.getId(), current, highest,
 206:                 progress, lockPrice, activation, state, hardProfitFloor, hardFloorTriggered, explanation);
 207:     }

 209:     /**
 210:      * FIX-120 correction/refinement to FIX-118. Called in the same transaction that persists the new TP.
 211:      * A TP change invalidates the prior executable lock as a calculation baseline. If current
 212:      * best progress remains at/above activation, stay ACTIVE but derive a fresh lock from the
 213:      * new geometry using raw extensionPrice as current and extensionHighest as the historical
 214:      * best seed. If progress falls below activation, preserve the historical lock for audit but
 215:      * make it non-executable in TP_EXTENSION_REBASE until the current geometry requalifies.
 216:      */
 217:     public ExtensionTransition onTakeProfitExtended(WalletManagedPosition position, BigDecimal newTarget, BigDecimal extensionPrice, Instant changedAt) {
 218:         if (position == null || newTarget == null || position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt() == null) {
 219:             return ExtensionTransition.none();
 220:         }
 221:         ProfitLockState previousState = normalizedState(position);
 222:         BigDecimal oldTarget = position.getTakeProfitUsdt();
 223:         BigDecimal oldLock = position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt();
 224:         BigDecimal extensionHighest = position.getHighestPriceUsdt() == null
 225:                 ? position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt()
 226:                 : position.getHighestPriceUsdt();
 227:         if (extensionPrice != null && extensionPrice.compareTo(extensionHighest) > 0) {
 228:             extensionHighest = extensionPrice;
 229:             position.setHighestPriceUsdt(extensionHighest);
 230:         }
 231:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), newTarget, extensionHighest);
 232:         BigDecimal activation = configuredProfile(settings()).activationPercent();
 233:         ProfitLockState nextState = previousState;
 234:         // TP changed now, so persist progress against the new geometry immediately even though
 235:         // the extension tick returns before the next normal Profit Lock evaluation.
 236:         position.setProfitLockProgressPercent(progress);
 237: 
 238:         if (previousState == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE) {
 239:             if (progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 240:                 nextState = ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 241:                 position.setProfitLockState(nextState);
 242:                 position.setProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(changedAt == null ? Instant.now() : changedAt);
 243:                 // Legacy compatibility flag deliberately stays true; ProfitLockState is the sole
 244:                 // authority for whether the persisted historical lock may execute.
 245:                 position.setProfitLockActive(true);
 246:             } else {
 247:                 // FIX-120: the extension still leaves the winner beyond activation, but the old
 248:                 // lock belongs to the superseded TP geometry. Establish a fresh geometry baseline.
 249:                 // Deliberately pass raw extensionPrice as current and extensionHighest as previous
 250:                 // highest: ProfitLockPolicy then sees the real boundary tick plus the authoritative
 251:                 // best price, while previousActive/previousLock are intentionally discarded.
 252:                 ProfitLockProfile profile = configuredProfile(settings());
 253:                 BigDecimal currentForPolicy = extensionPrice == null ? extensionHighest : extensionPrice;
 254:                 ProfitLockPolicy.State rebased = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 255:                         position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), newTarget, currentForPolicy, extensionHighest,
 256:                         false, null, true, profile.activationPercent(), profile.initialLockPercent(), profile.trailStepPercent());
 257:                 nextState = rebased.active() ? ProfitLockState.ACTIVE : ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 258:                 position.setHighestPriceUsdt(rebased.highestPrice());
 259:                 position.setProfitLockProgressPercent(rebased.progressPercent());
 260:                 position.setProfitLockPriceUsdt(rebased.lockPrice());
 261:                 position.setProfitLockState(nextState);
 262:                 position.setProfitLockActive(true);
 263:                 position.setProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(nextState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 264:                         ? (changedAt == null ? Instant.now() : changedAt)
 265:                         : null);
 266:                 progress = rebased.progressPercent();
 267:             }
 268:         } else if (previousState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE) {
 269:             // Repeated TP extensions keep the rebase active and refresh progress against the
 270:             // newest geometry without resetting the original rebase start timestamp.
 271:         }
 272: 
 273:         log.info("[FIX-120][PRODUCTION][TP_GEOMETRY_REBASE] positionId={}, symbol={}, previousState={}, newState={}, " +
 274:                         "oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, extensionPrice={}, highest={}, newGeometryProgressPct={}, oldLock={}, freshLock={}, " +
 275:                         "activationPct={}, geometryRebased={}",
 276:                 position.getId(), position.getSymbol(), previousState, nextState, oldTarget, newTarget,
 277:                 extensionPrice, position.getHighestPriceUsdt(), progress,
 278:                 oldLock, position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt(),
 279:                 activation, previousState == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE);

  16: /**
  17:  * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
  18:  *
  19:  * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
  20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
  21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
  22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
  23:  */
  24: @Component
  25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
  26: 
  27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
  28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
  29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
  30: 
  31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
  32: 
  33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
  34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
  38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
  40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
  41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
  43:                 .toList();
  44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
  45:         return new Scope();
  46:     }

  48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
  49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
  50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
  51:         State s=required();
  52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
  53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
  54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
  55:     }
  56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
  58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
  59:     }

 100:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;

 106:     public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
 107:         State s = required();
 108:         Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
 109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();
 110:         return s.signals.stream()
 111:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 112:                 .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
 113:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
 114:                 .limit(limit)
 115:                 .toList();
 116:     }

 125:      * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
 126:      * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
 127:      * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
 128:      */
 129:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
 130:         State s = required();

 133:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 134:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
 135:                 .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
 136:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
 137:     }

 160:      *
 161:      * This is deliberately NOT equivalent to analyze(indicator), which uses Instant.now().
 162:      * Using current context to reconstruct a 12:21 candle at 12:30 would create a synthetic
 163:      * signal that Production could never have produced at 12:21 and would break Replay parity.
 164:      * The same buildSignal(...) implementation is shared; only temporal authority differs.

 166:     @Transactional
 167:     public TradeSignal analyzeRecovered(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant evaluationTime) {
 168:         if (indicator == null) {
 169:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Recovered technical indicator is required");
 170:         }
 171:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
 172:         Instant asOf = evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime;
 173:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, asOf, true, false);
 174:         signal.setSignalMode("HISTORICAL_ONLY");
 175:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
 176:     }

 179:      * Regression-only analysis path. Runs the same production scoring/final-decision
 180:      * code but returns an unsaved TradeSignal so replay can never write to trade_signal.

 182:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 183:     public TradeSignal analyzeForRegression(IndicatorSnapshot snapshot, Instant evaluationTime) {
 184:         if (snapshot == null) {

 186:         }
 187:         ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler = replayAnalysisProfiler.get();
 188:         long startedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 189:         try {
 190:             return buildSignal(snapshot, evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime, true, true);
 191:         } finally {
 192:             if (profiler != null) {
 193:                 profiler.recordCall(System.nanoTime() - startedNs);
 194:             }
 195:         }
 196:     }

 245: 
 246:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();

 248:         MomentumBreakdown momentumBreakdown = momentumScore(i);
 249:         BandsVolumeBreakdown bandsVolume = bandsVolumeScore(i, previous);
 250:         int trend = movingAverages.total();
 251:         int volume = bandsVolume.total();

 255:         int fundamentals = fundamentalAvailable ? fundamentalBreakdown.total() : 0;

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/replay/ReplayAccessPolicy.java:1-160 =====
   1: package com.crypto.regression.replay;
   2: 
   3: import java.util.Locale;
   4: import java.util.Set;
   5: 
   6: /**
   7:  * FIX-156: central allow-list for Replay-owned SQL names. This is deliberately
   8:  * independent from Production repositories and must be used by Replay-local stores.
   9:  */
  10: public final class ReplayAccessPolicy {
  11:     private static final Set<String> REPLAY_TABLES = Set.of(
  12:             "replay_policy_profile",
  13:             "replay_fixture",
  14:             "replay_run_output",
  15:             "replay_decision_trace");
  16: 
  17:     private ReplayAccessPolicy() {
  18:     }
  19: 
  20:     public static void requireReplayTable(String table) {
  21:         String normalized = table == null ? "" : table.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
  22:         if (!REPLAY_TABLES.contains(normalized)) {
  23:             throw new ReplayForbiddenDependencyException("table=" + table);
  24:         }
  25:     }
  26: }

   8:  * Production continues to read its live configuration through its own paths.
   9:  */
  10: public record ReplayPolicyProfile(
  11:         long runId,
  12:         String profileHash,
  13:         Instant effectiveAt,
  14:         boolean dynamicProfitLockEnabled,
  15:         BigDecimal profitLockActivationPercent,
  16:         BigDecimal profitLockInitialPercent,
  17:         BigDecimal profitLockTrailStepPercent,
  18:         BigDecimal fixedNotionalUsdt) {
  19: 
  20:     public ReplayPolicyProfile {
  21:         if (profileHash == null || profileHash.isBlank()) {
  22:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-156 Replay policy profile hash is required");
  23:         }
  24:         if (effectiveAt == null || fixedNotionalUsdt == null || fixedNotionalUsdt.signum() <= 0) {
  25:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-156 Replay policy profile is incomplete");
  26:         }
  27:     }
  28: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/replay/ReplayPolicyProfileStore.java:1-160 =====
   1: package com.crypto.regression.replay;
   2: 
   3: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;
   5: 
   6: import java.sql.Timestamp;
   7: 
   8: /** FIX-156: Replay-only persistence for an immutable historical policy profile. */
   9: @Repository
  10: public class ReplayPolicyProfileStore {
  11:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  12: 
  13:     public ReplayPolicyProfileStore(JdbcTemplate jdbc) {
  14:         this.jdbc = jdbc;
  15:     }
  16: 
  17:     public ReplayPolicyProfile require(long runId) {
  18:         // FIX-156: never fall back to wallet_settings or current administration values.
  19:         ReplayAccessPolicy.requireReplayTable("replay_policy_profile");
  20:         return jdbc.query("""
  21:                 SELECT run_id, profile_hash, effective_at, dynamic_profit_lock_enabled,
  22:                        profit_lock_activation_percent, profit_lock_initial_percent,
  23:                        profit_lock_trail_step_percent, fixed_notional_usdt
  24:                 FROM replay_policy_profile WHERE run_id=?
  25:                 """, rs -> rs.next() ? new ReplayPolicyProfile(
  26:                 rs.getLong("run_id"), rs.getString("profile_hash"),
  27:                 rs.getTimestamp("effective_at").toInstant(),
  28:                 rs.getBoolean("dynamic_profit_lock_enabled"),
  29:                 rs.getBigDecimal("profit_lock_activation_percent"),
  30:                 rs.getBigDecimal("profit_lock_initial_percent"),
  31:                 rs.getBigDecimal("profit_lock_trail_step_percent"),
  32:                 rs.getBigDecimal("fixed_notional_usdt")) : null, runId);
  33:     }
  34: 
  35:     public void insert(ReplayPolicyProfile profile) {
  36:         // FIX-156: fixtures are prepared outside the Replay execution boundary.
  37:         ReplayAccessPolicy.requireReplayTable("replay_policy_profile");
  38:         int inserted = jdbc.update("""
  39:                 INSERT INTO replay_policy_profile
  40:                 (run_id, profile_hash, effective_at, dynamic_profit_lock_enabled,
  41:                  profit_lock_activation_percent, profit_lock_initial_percent,
  42:                  profit_lock_trail_step_percent, fixed_notional_usdt)
  43:                 VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
  44:                 """, profile.runId(), profile.profileHash(), Timestamp.from(profile.effectiveAt()),
  45:                 profile.dynamicProfitLockEnabled(), profile.profitLockActivationPercent(),
  46:                 profile.profitLockInitialPercent(), profile.profitLockTrailStepPercent(),
  47:                 profile.fixedNotionalUsdt());
  48:         if (inserted != 1) {
  49:             throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-156 could not persist Replay policy profile");
  50:         }
  51:     }
  52: }

   1: -- FIX-156: additive Replay-owned evidence only; no existing table is altered.
   2: CREATE TABLE replay_policy_profile (
   3:     run_id BIGINT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
   4:     profile_hash VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
   5:     effective_at TIMESTAMP(6) NOT NULL,
   6:     dynamic_profit_lock_enabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL,

   8:     profit_lock_initial_percent DECIMAL(12,6) NOT NULL,

  10:     fixed_notional_usdt DECIMAL(30,12) NOT NULL,
  11:     created_at TIMESTAMP(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  12:     INDEX idx_replay_policy_profile_hash (profile_hash)
  13: );
  14:

  26:

===== agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md:1-80 =====
   1: # Replay / Production Business-Logic-Parity Redesign
   2: 
   3: **Revision:** RPR-0.4-DRAFT  
   4: **Status:** DRAFT ƒ?" NOT APPROVED ƒ?" IMPLEMENTATION FORBIDDEN  
   5: **Created:** 2026-10-10  
   6: **Scope owner:** Codex implementation / Claude independent review
   7: 
   8: ## 1. Business objective
   9: 
  10: Make manual Replay reproduce the current paper-trading Production business flow
  11: as closely as durable historical evidence permits, while remaining completely
  12: isolated from Production trading state and wallet data.
  13: 
  14: The exact intended flow is:
  15: 
  16: ```text
  17: historical candle + historical context + canonical accepted price events
  18:   -> shared Production indicator/signal/decision policies
  19:   -> replay-local virtual wallet/account state
  20:   -> shared sizing/allocation/entry revalidation policies
  21:   -> replay-local position lifecycle
  22:   -> shared TP/SL/profit-lock/continuation/exit policies
  23:   -> replay output + explicit parity certificate
  24: ```
  25: 
  26: Replay must never write `wallet_*`, `paper_position`, `trade_signal`,
  27: `signal_processing_work`, or any other Production trading table.
  28: 
  29: The result is not a claim that historical Java scheduling, MySQL lock timing,
  30: network delay, external Binance availability, or the historical Production
  31: wallet balance can be reproduced. It is a verified **business-logic parity**
  32: claim only when all required market/decision evidence exists. Otherwise Replay
  33: remains available as a labelled simulation and may not claim parity.
  34: 
  35: ## 2. Confirmed root cause and evidence
  36: 
  37: The 2026-10-10 source audit established the following:
  38: 
  39: 1. Signal, entry, normal-exit, continuation, profit-lock, and near-TP policy
  40:    use several shared Production services.
  41: 2. `ShadowProductionReplayService` separately owns replay wallet cash,
  42:    positions, daily budget, persistence, price-loop lifecycle, and result
  43:    labels. It starts at `INITIAL_CAPITAL=10000`, runs one symbol/position, and
  44:    does not reproduce Production wallet/global controls.
  45: 3. A run is labelled `EXACT_PRICE_REPLAY` when at least one accepted price
  46:    event exists, rather than proving coverage for every interval while a
  47:    position was open.
  48: 4. Historical derivatives information is not persisted;
  49:    `DerivativesPositioningService.evaluateHistorical()` returns UNAVAILABLE.
  50:    Historical order-book evaluation can likewise be unavailable when snapshots
  51:    were not retained.
  52: 5. Replay-only FIX-11K and FIX-11Q observers, and the pre-FIX-122 UI revision,
  53:    have no current Production equivalent and must not contaminate parity runs.
  54: 6. Current Replay reads `WalletSettingsRepository` directly and its near-TP
  55:    path can call `BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy`, which can request a current
  56:    live Binance minimum notional. Both violate replay account/external-input
  57:    isolation.
  58: 
  59: ## 3. Boundaries
  60: 
  61: ### In scope
  62: 
  63: - Replay input/evidence qualification and run-level parity status.
  64: - Replay-local virtual wallet state, wallet controls, positions, executions,
  65:   daily statistics, and global limit reproduction.
  66: - Canonical price-event coverage qualification and event ordering.
  67: - Historical context-evidence qualification for derivatives and order book.
  68: - Removal of approved retired Replay-only business/experiment behaviour.
  69: - Parity certificate, diagnostics, migrations, tests, documentation.
  70: 
  71: ### Out of scope
  72: 
  73: - Any Production strategy change, sizing change, exit change, or wallet change.
  74: - Live-exchange orders, fills, fees, slippage, or order-book execution modelling.
  75: - Replaying database locks, thread scheduling, JVM pauses, WebSocket transport,
  76:   external REST failures, or transaction retries as historical facts.
  77: - FIX-153, FIX-154, and FIX-155 trading-policy changes.
  78: - Deleting Production evidence or existing historical Replay/archive rows.
  79: 
  80: ## 4. Contractual modes

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java:1-92 =====
   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import com.crypto.execution.domain.ExecutionOpportunity;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: 
   7: import java.time.Instant;
   8: import java.util.ArrayList;
   9: import java.util.Comparator;
  10: import java.util.List;
  11: import java.util.Optional;
  12: import java.util.HashSet;
  13: import java.util.Set;
  14: import java.util.function.Consumer;
  15: 
  16: /**
  17:  * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
  18:  *
  19:  * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
  20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
  21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
  22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
  23:  */
  24: @Component
  25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
  26: 
  27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
  28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
  29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
  30: 
  31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
  32: 
  33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
  34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
  38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
  40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
  41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
  43:                 .toList();
  44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
  45:         return new Scope();
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
  49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
  50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
  51:         State s=required();
  52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
  53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
  54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
  55:     }
  56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
  58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
  59:     }
  60:     public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
  61:     public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
  62:     public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
  63: 
  64:     public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
  65:     public long runId() { return required().runId; }
  66:     public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
  67:     public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
  68:     public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
  69: 
  70:     public void reference(Instant reference) { required().reference = reference; }
  71: 
  72:     /** FIX-056: expose the exact latest replay market-price event to the shared execution-price authority. */
  73:     public void referencePrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {
  74:         State s = required();

## OBJECTIONS

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** A static reachability scan alone cannot establish the requested boundary: it can miss dynamic SQL, JPA access, Spring proxies, and asynchronous scope loss. The contract needs both static checks and direct/async context tests that prove no publication after forbidden account, HTTP, or shared-write access.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Refusals/exclusions can inflate a parity percentage unless the cohort denominator, refusal taxonomy, and minimum coverage are frozen before the sealed dataset is opened.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** V110 is Replay-owned in purpose, but it is a Flyway migration in the shared application schema. The owner must explicitly decide whether that schema mutation is permitted under “Replay-only/zero Production code,” and record the deployment behavior. It must not be silently treated as no Production impact.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No tests, build, database query, service, Production system, or private log was accessed in this review.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The complete external RPR-0.9.12 difference register—especially D02–D29 and S01–S14—is unavailable in this workspace, so it cannot be independently frozen from this preparation alone.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The untracked FIX-156 artifacts may be prior work; their presence does not prove they were run, applied, or approved.
